- 2026-10-02 · 6.7 · Build decisions (BD-67-1..24), for ruling. 1 Names as built (plan §0.3): restrictedTender, restrictionCertSighted/ValidUntil, UNLOCKED_FOR_OPENING, Award.awardeePartyId. 2 The box is judged by the LOCKED event's occurredAt, which 6.6 writes as the server's stamp (C-3 says recordedAt; the two are the same instant for a LOCKED event). 3 An undecided late envelope does not block finalising the main opening (it is never openable there); the award gate holds it (6.6 LATE_UNDECIDED, then ADMITTED_UNOPENED once carried). 4 A panel is drawn from every seat in force (voting, alternates, the secretary), each resolved to its one holder; a vacant or contested seat holds nobody; the seat is snapshotted as its office, its label or its position. 5 Presence is fixed when the opening starts; an absent member neither authorises, initials nor signs; at least two present. 6 The independence rule excludes the named unit and every unit under it (path through the structure), beyond C-6's "the unit". 7 An invited exercise's bidder is checked against its letters of invitation (NOT_ON_INVITEE_LIST without one); the INVITEES reading counts letters, PURCHASERS distinct parties holding a live sale. 8 The opening committee, the unpurchased-bid consequence and the estimate's requirement are read live when the opening starts and frozen on it (not at publication as 6.6 CR-2); an unpurchased consequence unset at start but set since is recorded on the opening, audited, the first time a verdict needs it. 9 A verdict is only on eliminatory grounds; there is no free "other" rejection (a concern goes in the minutes); a changed checklist answer clears the verdict. 10 A correction asks every present member's initial again and has the price entered afresh (the drawer never shows it back). 11 A supplementary opening uses the main opening's checklist and rules; its panel rules are read when it starts. 12 One copy explanation per opening. 13 returnedByBidder unset on an approved pack has no exit (CR-2 precedent): finalising names Configuration › Tender documents; drafts refresh; the declaration is returned by definition. 14 A voluntary estimate's paper copy may be left unanswered (no comparison then); a required one must be answered. 15 An opening with no offer finalises (an empty box); the exit from a failed tender is cancellation (C-9). 16 The opening's settings sit on Configuration › Invitation (an Opening card with its own count, the window badge unchanged), the panel on each committee in its own drawer with a reason, the unit on the person (optional on the Person type so no fixture changes; the drawer comparator records the new field as decided). 17 The tab is "Bids and opening", but two 6.6 texts locked by existing tests still say "the Bids received tab" (committee/labels.ts LATE_TABLED_TEXT, the award's late-envelope fault); left unchanged, rewording to "the Received section of Bids and opening" would change committee/labels.test.ts and award/bids-gate.test.ts. 18 The print before finalising shows the offers read so far with the estimate sealed, watermarked "Not finalised" (the panel signs that paper; plan §5 said "frozen rows only"). 19 The paper copy's figure is audited under a new registered SULIT key, estimatePaperCopy. 20 Demo per OD-4 A: REPORT, so no demo offer is rejected (REJECT covered by tests and the verify run); demo_aw7 now names Contoh Bina (demo_pty2), carried, at its price as read; "Demo Network Builders" is a bidder named at opening. 21 History still names 6.6's envelope rows "Record" (a 6.6 gap, not changed); the opening's rows have their names. 22 The Grand Summary shows the English words on screen; the Malay ones are kept for a bilingual print. 23 The panel and who is present are two multi-selects in the start drawer (the shared multi-select posts newline-joined; the action splits it). 24 The demo wipe removes a box envelope with its opening (the CHECK) and clears an award's awardee link before the parties go. Questions for RSAJ: OD-4 (eliminate or report a bid with no purchase), OD-9 (how the tenderers invited are counted), the independence rule (FR-CTE-011), which parts a bidder returns. Spec: FR-OPN-001–011, FR-CTE-011, OD-1..OD-11, C-1..C-13.

- 2026-10-05 · Founder: the requisition's Register step is removed. It mirrored the finance system's registration, which minted the numbers the customer does not use (removed earlier the same day), so after them it recorded and checked nothing. An endorsed requisition now goes straight to the budget check (Endorsed to Budget checked or Over budget); nothing is newly registered. The Registered state stays for requisitions registered before, and they go on to their budget check as before. Committee matters waiting for a requisition are now linked at endorsement, where registration used to link them.

- 2026-10-05 · Founder, committee sittings: (1) attendance is a Present / Absent toggle on each member's line, recorded through the same action and rules (an alternate still picks the seat it stands in for in its drawer). (2) The vote buttons are English only, Agree / Disagree (the Malay words stay in the label table). (3) The minutes are two files on the sitting, the minutes and the signed copy, each uploaded and downloaded on its line: new attachment purpose SITTING_MINUTES (minutes), open once the sitting has started and correctable as a new version after minuting. Minuting needs the minutes file, or minutes typed before uploads existed, which still show as typed. The compact panel keeps the access statement behind the information button. Local .env had no DOCUMENT_* settings, so every upload was refused; copied from .env.example (local only).

- 2026-10-05 · Founder: the anti-splitting check (FR-REQ-015) is removed from requisitions and Requisition controls. Submission no longer assesses or stores a split check, final approval no longer waits for a justification (so a committee citation, including the auto-link from a sitting's outcome, is never refused for it), and the Justify act, the Splitting check card, the approvers-saw row, the list's "possible splits" count and the four settings are gone. It never changed a route. Kept: the stored checks, the schema columns and the History words, so past records still read in words. Dead now: src/lib/requisition/splitting.ts (except splitStateLabel for History) and the policy columns, logged in the backlog.

- 2026-10-05 · Founder: the budget code is mandatory on a requisition raised in the portal, as the cost centre and account are (required to save a draft, endorse and submit). The form offers only the budget codes on the chosen account. A requisition received from the finance system (intake, no caller now) is exempt: it carries the finance system's own budget figures.

- 2026-10-05 · Founder, new sourcing exercise: (1) the picker lists only requisitions an exercise can be opened on, (2) the value hint shows only where part of the requisition is already in other exercises, (4) no "How this will run" panel (proposals show on their fields), (3) the scope split is one "What is delivered" choice covering the whole value, from which the contract type is still derived. Mixed scope is no longer split by value on the form; a draft saved with several parts opens on its largest and saving records that one part. Exercises already saved keep their parts as recorded.

- 2026-10-05 · Founder, sourcing exercise screen simplified (UI only except where stated): Classification and Contract type are replaced by an Overview (process-flow stepper, approval-for-tendering stepper, contract card, Details & compliance, Key dates & person in charge). Scope and estimate stays (the server still requires the bill, panel and estimate before approval for tendering) but its readiness card and the sealed-storage note are removed (founder chose delete). Tender documents: the "Before it can go for approval" list is hidden completely (founder chose hide over a short list; the server still refuses and says why on submit), "What the contract type asks" removed, assurance shown only when the policy asks it, mandatory clauses as one line, the quotation document folded into part 1 (Notice of quotation), the tendering route shown as a stepper. Invitation: briefing and site visit can be scheduled before approval; rule readouts behind an information button. Backend addition (founder chose this pass): person in charge and work code on the exercise.

- 2026-10-06 · Founder: a requisition's estimated value is the total of its lines, worked out on the form as they are typed or uploaded; it is no longer typed. The action still requires the two to agree.

- 2026-10-06 · Founder: the need statement is removed from the requisition form and is no longer required. One recorded before is kept as it was (shown on the requisition and printed on the PR only where it exists); the column stays.
- 2026-10-06 · Founder: on the requisition form the requesting officer is chosen first, and their unit fills in the department, section and cost centre (only fields still empty or filled by an earlier officer; a cost centre only where exactly one carries the unit).
- 2026-10-06 · Founder: marking a requisition endorsed now checks its budget automatically where the check needs nothing typed (the portal ledger), and so does changing its funding route; the Check budget button stays for a re-check and for typed figures. The checking rules live in one function used by all three.
- 2026-10-06 · Founder: choosing the officer fills section and cost centre only under the department the form ends up with (never another department's), and the note names only the fields actually filled, kept or left. Open for decision: with the portal ledger as budget source, Mark endorsed now carries the requisition straight to Budget checked, so the Return as incomplete step (FR-REQ-010, Endorsed only) is no longer reachable; the Mark endorsed drawer says so. Options: allow Return from Budget checked / Over budget, or a Requisition controls switch for checking at endorsement.

- 2026-10-06 · Founder: with the budget checked automatically at endorsement, "Return as incomplete" (FR-REQ-010) stays available after the check (Budget checked, Over budget, and Registered left from before) until the requisition is submitted.

- 2026-10-06 · Founder: the supplier portal's public pages (home, opportunities, results, registration application) are built without sign-in; this lifts the "vendor portal waits for sign-in" rule (OD-1 B, and the FR-VND-001 self-registration move at line 810) for those pages only. My Bids, My Projects and Company stay placeholders that say they arrive with sign-in. Nothing is stubbed: no account, token or magic link.
- 2026-10-06 · Supplier portal home: "How to take part" says only what the product itself fixes (bids are paper envelopes, as EnvelopeChannel records; no electronic submission exists) and points at each opportunity's notice for the fee, the places and the times, which are set per exercise window, never per organisation. No tender-box place or fee is written on the home page.
- 2026-10-06 · Supplier portal words are `Words` {en, ms} in src/lib/portal/labels.ts (reusing `w` from contract/labels) and rendered as `.en`: there is no language toggle, and none is built for the portal alone.
- 2026-10-06 · Supplier workspace: the topbar bell dot (the staff's pending committee decisions) is not shown on supplier pages, and the supplier "projects" crumb reads "My Projects", not the staff word "Contracts". Not changed: the workspace switcher still lets anyone reach the staff side (an access matter that arrives with sign-in).
- 2026-10-06 · Public opportunities: an exercise is public only when its window's own path is ADVERTISED and records a publication; the method's flags are not read, because they say what the method would do, not what happened. No window means not public. The rule (`isPublicOpportunity`, src/lib/portal/public.ts) switches over every ExerciseStatus by name, so a state added later fails to compile until decided.
- 2026-10-06 · Two "restricted" flags, two meanings: `SourcingExercise.restricted` (the method's confidential flag, FR-MTD-007) always hides the exercise from the public. `restrictedTender` (a restriction on bidders the notice states) never hides it: it shows as "Restriction on bidders" in the organisation's own words (TenderDocumentPolicy.restrictionLabelEn), with a plain fallback sentence where none is set.
- 2026-10-06 · Opportunities board has no "recently closed" cutoff, because a day count is policy and no setting exists for one. It lists every public exercise in views Open (the default), Closed and Cancelled. A closed exercise stays under Closed until an award is issued or accepted, then it leaves the board for Results. If a cutoff is wanted it needs an organisation setting.
- 2026-10-06 · After the closing every stage reads simply "Closed" to the public (opening, under evaluation, evaluated): those stages are not public. A cancelled exercise is shown only if it had been published on the advertised path, and only as "Cancelled" with the day the cancellation took effect. Its reason, fee, notice, events and addenda are not shown.
- 2026-10-06 · No office contact is shown on opportunity pages: there is no setting that marks one public. The only places and hours shown are those in the published notice's own fields (where to buy, inspect and hand in bids, ways to pay), which the notice already prints.
- 2026-10-06 · Public pages read through one narrow select (src/lib/portal/select.ts) and a projection that returns only strings, numbers and flags. The test scans the select for every SULIT field and every private relation. "Mandatory" for a briefing is read from the policy the window was published under, never today's configuration.
